def c5_from_h(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)

   - repair_to_sum(h, m): project to bounds and exact sum (within 1e-15). Method: h = np.clip(h,0,1); adjust via water-filling to sum m.

   - Use h0, n=len. Minimize c5 with bounds and sum eq. Use finite diff. Since c5 non-smooth, maybe wrapper with smoothing optional.
   - Repair result; evaluate. Add if better.
   - Use top candidate to upsample to higher n and refine.
4. Binary local search maybe using candidate round to binary? But parent may not binary; rounding may break. Could generate binary candidates separately.

Could use a custom active-set trust region by scipy.optimize.minimize with a linear/quadratic objective? Let's examine.

At point h near optimum, active set A where F_l = f. We can solve a convex? The minmax of active quadratics? Each active F_l is concave in h (negative products) plus linear. def lag_values(h):
  pos = np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n)
  for e in range(n):
    d=n-e
    pos[e]=np.dot(h[:d], 1-h[e:])
    if e>0: neg[e]=np.dot(h[e:],1-h[:d])
  return pos, neg

Gradient function for lag:
def grad_lag_active(h,e,is_pos,dx):
   n=len; g=zeros(n)
   if is_pos:
      d=n-e
      g[:d] += 1.0
      if e>0:
         g[e:] -= h[:-e]
   else:
      d=n-e
      # g[e:] += 1 - h[:d]
      if d>0 and e<n:
         g[e:] += (1.0 - h[:d])
      g[:d] -= h[e:]
   return g*dx

B_e = ∑_{j=0}^{d-1} h_{j+e} (1 - h_j) = ∑ h_{j+e} - ∑ h_{j+e} h_j.
Variable y_i:
1) As h_{j+e}: i=j+e, j=i-e, range j=0..d-1 => i=e..n-1. Derivative: from linear +1, from product -h_j = -h_{i-e}. So contribution +(1-h_{i-e}).
2) As h_j: j=i, range i=0..d-1. In product -h_{i+e}h_i, derivative -h_{i+e}. No linear from -h_j. So contribution -h_{i+e}.
Total: g_i = 1_{i≥e}(1-h_{i-e}) - 1_{i≤d-1}h_{i+e}.
For e=0: g_i= (1-h_i) - h_i =1-2h_i. OK.
For pos: A_e = ∑_{i=0}^{d-1} h_i(1-h_{i+e}) = ∑ h_i - ∑ h_i h_{i+e}.

Let's check! A_e = ∑_{u=0}^{d-1} h_u - ∑_{u=0}^{d-1} h_u h_{u+e}. A variable i appears in first sum if i≤d-1: coefficient 1. Optimization step:
def projected_subgradient_refine(h, max_iter=2000, time_limit=None, tol=1e-8):
  n=len; m=n/2; dx=2/n
  h=repair(h,m)
  pos,neg=lag_values(h); fcount=max(max); f=dx*fcount
  best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
  step=0.05
  stuck=0
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time and elapsed>time: break
    pos,neg=lag_values(h)
    fcount=max; f=dx*fcount
    # if f < best by >? update
    if f < best_f - 1e-12: best_f=f; best_h=h.copy; stuck=0 else stuck++
    # If stuck too many, random perturb best or reduce step.
    if stuck > 100: h=best_h.copy(); step=max(0.005, step*0.5); add small noise? 
    active... grad...
    norm= np.linalg.norm(g)
    if norm < 1e-12: break
    # normalize direction maybe
    g = g / (norm + 1e-12)
    # Projected gradient descent
    for trial step in [step, step*0.5, ...]
      y=h - trial*g
      hp=project(y)
      pos2,neg2=lag_values(hp); f2=dx*max
      if f2 < f - 1e-12:
         h=hp; step=min(0.2, step*1.05); break
      # if no decrease: step*=0.5
    if step < 1e-6: possibly noise
  return best

Let's write iterative active selection without lambda:
def act_weights(vals, fcount, tol_frac=0.05):
    tol = max(1e-9, tol_frac * fcount)
    idx=np.where(vals >= fcount - tol)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(vals))])
    w=np.exp((vals[idx]-fcount)/max(tol,1e-9))  # for values below max; since vals<=fcount, exp<=1.
    wsum=w.sum()
    if wsum<=0: w=np.ones_like(w)
    return idx, w/wsum

Let's design final code with several phases:
- initialize candidates list with parent, sym, binary variants, maybe upsampled versions.
- Phase 1: For each candidate with time per <=20s, run `gradient_descent` (smooth) to improve. Use top one first.
- Phase 2: Upsample top best to larger n (e.g., 100, 140, 200) by step-repeat; run gradient_descent. Since continuous optimum may have finer features.
- Phase 3: Random noisy starts from best and random binary (if time) with short GD.
- Phase 4: Polish best using active gradient very small steps.
- Return best.

def current_time(): return time.time()

def c5_from_h(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 1e9
    dx=2.0/n
    # clip? objective assumes [0,1], but if tiny out, clip? For evaluation exact returned h should be within. For internal, use h as is? Clip to [0,1] maybe hides violations. We'll maintain.
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    val=float(np.max(corr))*dx
    if val <0: return 0.0
    return val

def lag_values(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=len(h)
    oh=1.0-h
    pos=np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n)
    pos[0]=np.dot(h,oh)
    neg[0]=pos[0]
    # Precompute? Loop e 1..n-1
    for e in range(1,n):
        d=n-e
        pos[e]=np.dot(h[:d], oh[e:])
        neg[e]=np.dot(h[e:], oh[:d])
    return pos,neg
Could use np.dot full loops; for e=n? no.

def project_box_sum(h, target):
    y=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    # quick if sum target and bounds ok? use projection anyway.
    lo=float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi=float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    # sum at lo should be n (all 1), hi 0. But if target outside [0,n], clamp.
    target=float(np.clip(target,0.0,float(len(y))))
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if s > target:
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
    nu=(lo+hi)*0.5
    z=np.clip(y-nu,0.0,1.0)
    # final correction to adjust sum exactly
    diff=target - float(np.sum(z))
    tol=1e-14
    if diff > tol:
       # add to coordinates with largest values (closest to 1) first? To stay in bounds.
       order=np.argsort(z)[::-1]
       rem=diff
       for idx in order:
          space=1.0 - z[idx]
          if space > 0.0:
             add = space if space < rem else rem
             z[idx] += add
             rem -= add
             if rem <= tol: break
    elif diff < -tol:
       order=np.argsort(z)
       rem=-diff
       for idx in order:
          space=z[idx]
          if space > 0.0:
             sub = space if space < rem else rem
             z[idx] -= sub
             rem -= sub
             if rem <= tol: break
    return z

Potential: If final correction pushes past bound due floating, maybe still tiny; clip after? Could check. But adding space exactly. Good.

def symmetrize(h):
    return (h + h[::-1]) * 0.5

def make_candidate_original(h):
    if h is None: return None
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    # if values not exact sum, repair to n/2 if even; if odd? target = round? But task requires n even. For fallback, maybe make n even.
    n=len(h)
    if n %2 ==1:
       # if odd, truncate or append? target n/2 noninteger; instead convert to even by repeating? We can just keep if initial odd? But returned must be even. Simpler: if odd, take first n-1 and repair to m=(n-1)/2.
       h=h[:-1]
    m=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,m)
    return h

Random balanced binary:
def random_balanced(n, seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   idx=rng.choice(n, n//2, replace=False)
   h=np.zeros(n); h[idx]=1.0; return h

def gradient_descent(h, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start=None, tau_end=0.2, alpha_start=0.05, verbose=False):
    n=len; target=n/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    # initial pos/neg/count
    pos,neg=lag_values(h); fcount=...; f=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
    if tau_start is None: tau_start=max(1.0, 0.25*fcount)?? If fcount 13, 3.25; maybe too smooth. Use max(1.0, 0.5) independent.
    tau=tau_start
    alpha=alpha_start
    min_alpha=1e-5
    stuck=0
    recent_improve=0
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()-start > time_limit: break
       # tau anneal: reduce slowly; maybe tau = max(tau_end, tau_start*(tau_end/tau_start)**(it/max_iter))? We can simply set based on elapsed fraction and stagnation.
       # Use tau = max(tau_end, tau_start * (0.99 ** it))
       pos,neg=lag_values(h)
       fcount=max(pos.max(), neg.max())
       f=dx*fcount
       if f < best_f - 1e-14:
          best_f=f; best_h=h.copy(); stuck=0; recent_improve+=1
       else:
          stuck+=1; recent_improve=0
       # If stuck > 50, small noise
       if stuck > 50:
          noise = 0.01 * (stuck//50)
          h = project_box_sum(best_h + noise*(rng.random(n)-0.5)*2, target)
          pos,neg=lag_values(h) ...
          # maybe after noise, reduce alpha
          alpha=max(min_alpha, alpha*0.5)
          stuck=0
       # gradient
       g=smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau)
       norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if norm < 1e-10:
          # maybe try random small noise
          h=project_box_sum(best_h + 0.005*(rng.random(n)-0.5), target)
          continue
       g /= norm
       accepted=False
       a=alpha
       for trial in range(8):
          hp=project_box_sum(h - a*g, target)
          # if hp identical? check
          f2=c5_from_h(hp)
          if f2 < f - 1e-14:
             h=hp; accepted=True; break
          a *= 0.5
          if a < min_alpha: break
       if accepted:
          alpha=min(0.2, alpha*1.1)
       else:
          alpha=max(min_alpha, alpha*0.6)
       # reduce tau if not improving in last 50
       if recent_improve==0 and (it % 50==49):
          tau=max(tau_end, tau*0.8)
       # if f nearly best and tau near end, reduce alpha
    # final polish? maybe return best_h,best_f
    return best_h,best_f

def smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau):

   W = wpos.sum()+wneg.sum()
   if W <= 1e-12: return zeros, fcount
   maxw=max(wpos.max(), wneg.max())

   idxn=np.where(wneg>thr)[0]
   for e in idxn:
      w=wneg[e]/W
      d=n-e
      g[e:] += w*(1-h[:d])
      g[:d] -= w*h[e:]
   return g

def projected_gradient_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau, alpha=0.05, seed=1):
    n=len; dx=2/n
    h=project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    pos,neg=lag_values(h); fcount=pos.max() if pos.max()>neg.max() else neg.max(); f=dx*fcount
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
    alpha_min=1e-5
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_dec = 0
    tau_min=max(0.2, 0.002*n?) Wait tau in counts, not per n. For n larger, min maybe 0.5. Let's pass tau_min.
    for it:
       if elapsed>time_limit: break
       pos,neg=lag_values(h)
       fcount=...
       f=...
       if f < best_f - 1e-12: update; no_dec=0
       else: no_dec+=1
       if no_dec > 80:
          # noise from best
          sigma = 0.005 + 0.005*min(4, no_dec//80)
          h = project_box_sum(best_h + sigma*(rng.random(n)-0.5)*2, target)
          no_dec=0
          alpha=max(alpha_min, alpha*0.7)
          pos,neg=lag_values(h); f...
       tau_eff = max(tau_min, tau * (0.98 ** it)) maybe. Or dynamic: if no_dec large reduce tau.
       g=smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau_eff)
       success=False
       if norm>...
          for dir in [g]:
             line search
          if not success:
             # active directions
             for e in top 3 pos/neg:
                g=grad_one; line search
       # if success accepted, alpha increase
       # if not, reduce alpha, tau
    return best_h,best_f

   return None,None,alpha/64,False

Binarize candidate:
def binarize_candidate(h, seed=0):
   n=len; m=n//2
   # start with threshold
   h2=(h>=0.5).astype(float)
   ones=int(h2.sum())
   if ones>m:
      # flip ones with h closest to 0.5 from above: smallest h among ones?
      idx=np.where(h2==1)[0]
      order=np.argsort(h[idx]) # ones closest to 0.5 have smaller values (but >=.5)
      for k in range(ones-m): h2[order[k]]=0
   elif ones<m:
      idx=np.where(h2==0)[0]
      order=np.argsort(-h[idx]) # zeros closest to .5 have larger h (<=.5)
      for k in range(m-ones): h2[order[k]]=1
   return h2

Binary local search algorithm:
def binary_local_search(h, time_limit, seed):
   n=len; m=n//2; target=m
   h=project_box_sum(h,target) # should binary if input
   # ensure binary? h = (h>0.5) then adjust? We'll initialize binary.
   current=c5_from_h(h)
   best_h=h; best_c=current
   rng=RandomState
   start=time
   no_improve=0
   temp? deterministic hill climb with random order.
   while elapsed<time and no_improve<limit:
       ones=np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]
       # Try random K pairs; if any improve, accept first, reset no_improve.
       found=False
       K=200? n=70 -> ones35 zeros35. Evaluate 200 pairs *5k=1M; okay per iter.
       for _ in range(K):
          a=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; b=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
          h[a]=0; h[b]=1; f=c5_from_h(h); h[a]=1; h[b]=0
          if f < current - 1e-12:
             h[a]=0;h[b]=1; current=f; found=True; break
       if found:
          if current < best_c: update; no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          # if stuck, do random perturb with temperature? Or same best and restart.
          if no_improve>20:
             # take 3 random swaps (preserve sum) from best, evaluate, choose best
             base=best_h.copy(); temp=0.02 (not used)
             for t in range(5):
                 cand=base.copy()
                 for r in range(3):
                    ones=... zeros=...
                    a=...;b=...
                    cand[a]=0;cand[b]=1
                 f=c5_from_h(cand)
                 if f<best_c: best_h=cand; best_c=f; h=cand.copy(); current=f; no_improve=0; break
             if no_improve>60: break
   return best_h,best_c

def pairwise_random_search(h, target, time_limit, seed):
   n=len; best=h; best_f=eval
   rng...
   while time:
      idxs=rng.choice(n,2,replace=False)
      i,j=...
      # feasible δ range: h_i + δ in [0,1], h_j - δ in[0,1] => δ∈[max(0,h_i-1), min(1,h_i) ∩ max(h_j-1,? Actually δ = new_i - old_i; new_j=old_j-δ. new_i=old_i+δ ∈[0,1] => δ∈[-old_i,1-old_i]; new_j=old_j-δ ∈[0,1] => δ∈[old_j-1, old_j]. Intersect.

1. `repair_h(h)`:

def repair_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size == 0: return None
    if h.size % 2 == 1:
        h = h[:-1]
    target = h.size / 2.0
    return project_box_sum(h, target)
```

5. `smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau)` as above. Need include pos[0] and neg>0. Compute weights. To avoid very small tau underflow causing W=0? Implementation details:
```
def smooth_gradient(h, pos, neg, tau):
    n = h.shape[0]
    fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    if tau <= 0: return np.zeros(n), fcount
    shifted = (pos - fcount) / tau
    shifted = np.where(shifted > 0.0, 0.0, shifted)
    wpos = np.exp(shifted)
    wneg = np.exp((neg - fcount)/tau) # similar
    wneg[0] = 0.0
    W = float(wpos.sum() + wneg.sum())
    g = np.zeros(n)
    if W <= 0: return g, fcount
    maxw = max(float(wpos.max()), float(wneg.max()))
    thr = 1e-7 * maxw
    idxp = np.where(wpos > thr)[0]
    for e in idxp:
       w = wpos[e] / W
       if w <= 0: continue
       d = n-e
       g[:d] += w * (1.0 - h[e:])
       if e > 0:
           g[e:] -= w * h[:d]
    idxn = np.where(wneg > thr)[0]
    for e in idxn:
       w=wneg[e]/W
       d=n-e
       g[e:] += w * (1.0 - h[:d])
       g[:d] -= w * h[e:]
    return g, fcount
```
Because `1.0 - h` alloc each loop; we can precompute `oh=1-h` outside and pass? To optimize:
In function, set oh=1.0-h once. Use oh slices. Good.
```
oh=1.0-h
g[:d] += w*oh[e:]
g[e:] -= w*h[:d]
```
Same neg.

6. `gradient_descent_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start, tau_end, alpha_start, seed)`:
Need avoid infinite noise. Let's write carefully.
```
def projected_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start, tau_end, alpha_start, seed):
    h = project_box_sum(h, target).copy()
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / n
    pos, neg = lag_values(h)
    fcount = max(pos.max(), neg.max())
    f = fcount*dx
    best_h = h.copy(); best_f = f
    tau = tau_start
    alpha = alpha_start
    alpha_min = 1e-5
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stalled = 0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time() - start > time_limit:
            break
        pos, neg = lag_values(h)
        fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
        f = fcount * dx
        if f < best_f - 1e-14:
            best_f = f; best_h = h.copy(); stalled=0
        else:
            stalled += 1
        if stalled > 80:
            sigma = 0.005 + 0.005 * min(5, stalled // 80)
            h = project_box_sum(best_h + sigma * (rng.random(n) - 0.5), target)
            pos, neg = lag_values(h)
            fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
            f = fcount*dx
            if f < best_f - 1e-14:
                best_f = f; best_h = h.copy()
            stalled = 0
            alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha * 0.7)
        tau_eff = max(tau_end, tau_start * (0.995 ** it))
        # But if it large, 0.995^10000 ~ e^-50 ~0; good. Alternatively reduce faster.
        g, fcount = smooth_gradient(h, pos, neg, tau_eff)
        accepted = False
        if np.linalg.norm(g) > 1e-12:
            gn = g / np.linalg.norm(g)
            a = alpha
            for _ in range(6):
                hp = project_box_sum(h - a * gn, target)
                f2 = objective_value(hp)
                if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                    h = hp; accepted=True; break
                a *= 0.5
            if not accepted:
                # try active directions
                for e in np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]:
                    if e==0: continue
                    g2 = grad_one(h,e,False)
                    if norm...
                for e in np.argsort(pos)[::-1][:3]: g2=grad_one True
        if accepted:
            alpha = min(0.2, alpha * 1.05)
        else:
            alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha * 0.65)
        # reduce tau if stalled
        if stalled > 0 and it % 100 == 99:
            tau_start = max(tau_end, tau_start * 0.85)  # modifying base? Maybe not.
```
We used `np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]` but neg[0] same as pos0; skip. For active directions, define `grad_one(h,e,positive)`:
```
def grad_one(h,e,positive):
   n=len; g=zeros; oh=1-h; d=n-e
   if positive:
      g[:d]+=oh[e:]
      if e>0: g[e:]-=h[:d]
   else:
      g[e:]+=oh[:d]
      g[:d]-=h[e:]
   return g
```
No dx factor; normalized.
When trying active, line search with same f_current. But if we update h inside direction loop, need update f? We can just try first successful and break.

7. `pairwise_random_search(h,target,time_limit,seed)`:

def pairwise_random_search(h,target,time_limit,seed):
    h=project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    best=h.copy(); best_f=objective_value(h)
    n=len; start=time; rng=...
    no_improve=0
    while time - start < time_limit:
       idx = rng.choice(n, 2, replace=False)
       i=int(idx[0]); j=int(idx[1])
       s=h[i]+h[j]
       lo=max(0.0, s-1.0); hi=min(1.0, s)
       if hi-lo <= 1e-14: continue
       v = lo + rng.random()*(hi-lo)
       cand=h.copy(); cand[i]=v; cand[j]=s-v
       # maybe also try small delta around current? Random enough.
       f=objective_value(cand)
       if f < best_f - 1e-12:
           best_h=cand; best_f=f; h=cand.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           if no_improve > 500:
               # perturb current 3 random pairs from best
               cand=best_h.copy()
               for _ in range(3):
                  idx=...
                  update cand
               if objective_value(cand) < best_f: accept; else h=best_h; no_improve=0? avoid infinite
               if no_improve > 2000: break
    return best,true
```
Need avoid c5 eval too many; time limit small. Pairwise search random may not find improvements in 30s due low acceptance; but can.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  state={'h':None,'c':1e9}
  candidates=[]
  rng_seed=seed
  # add initial and variants
  if 'initial_h_values' in globals():
      base = np.asarray(initial_h_values, float)
      for transform in [identity, sym, binarize, upsample?]: ...
  else:
      # create n=70 random binary + a few pattern candidates
  # best state set by add_candidate
  # Phase 1: refine best and next best with gradient
  order candidates by c
  for idx in range(min(3, len(candidates))):
      h=candidates[idx]
      n=len(h); m=n/2
      max_time = 250 if n<=80 else 150 if n<=160 else 80
      h2,c2 = projected_refine(h,m, max_iter=5000, time_limit=max_time, tau_start=..., tau_end=..., alpha_start=0.05, seed=1000+idx)
      add_candidate(h2)
      # if time left and c2 best, try pairwise
      if time_left > 50 and c2<state.c +:
         h3,c3=pairwise_random_search(h2,m,30,seed=2000+idx)
         add_candidate(h3)
  # Phase 2: upscale best to larger n
  base_best=state.h
  for n_new in [98, 140, 200, 300]:
     if time_left < 60: break
     h=upsample_step(base_best,n_new)
     # maybe also sym? add
     add_candidate(h)
     # refine if time
     max_time = ...
     h2,c2=projected_refine(h,n_new/2, time_limit=max_time, ...)
     add_candidate(h2)
  # Phase 3: random starts from best/fallback with noise
  for s in range(5): if time_left>30:
      h=project_box_sum(state.h + 0.03*(rng.random-0.5), m)
      add_candidate(h)
  # Phase 4: pairwise and polish best for remaining time
  if time_left>20:
      h2=pairwise_random_search(state.h, m, time_left=30, seed=...)
      add
  # final polish projected_refine small tau end
  if time_left>30:
      h2=projected_refine(state.h, m, max_iter=5000, time_limit=time_left-10, tau_start=0.5, tau_end=0.1, alpha=0.02)
      add
  # final repair and return
  h=project_box_sum(state.h, len(state.h)/2.0)
  c=objective_value(h)
  # final exact correction maybe
  return h,c,len(h)
```

```
def pairwise_random_search(h, target, time_limit, seed):
    h = project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    n=h.size
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=objective_value(h)
    start=time.time(); rng=RandomState(seed); no=0
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
        i,j = rng.choice(n,2,replace=False)
        i=int(i); j=int(j)
        s=h[i]+h[j]
        lo=max(0.0, s-1.0); hi=min(1.0, s)
        if hi-lo <= 1e-14:
            no+=1
            if no>1000: break
            continue
        vals=[]
        # add current? no change skip; random and extremes
        vals.append(lo + rng.random()*(hi-lo))
        if abs(lo - h[i]) > 1e-12: vals.append(lo)
        if abs(hi - h[i]) > 1e-12: vals.append(hi)
        vals.append(0.5*s) # midpoint
        cands=set? no.
        accepted=False
        for v in vals:
           vfloat=float(v)
           if vfloat < lo-1e-12 or vfloat > hi+1e-12: continue
           vfloat = min(1.0, max(0.0, vfloat))
           w = s - vfloat
           if w < 0 or w > 1: continue
           cand=h.copy(); cand[i]=vfloat; cand[j]=w
           f=objective_value(cand)
           if f < best_f - 1e-12:
               best_h=cand; best_f=f; h=cand; accepted=True; no=0; break
        if not accepted:
           no +=1
           if no > 2000:
              # perturb best with 2 random pair updates and continue
              cand=best_h.copy()
              for _ in range(2):
                 i2,j2 = rng.choice(n,2,replace=False)
                 s2=h? use cand values
                 lo2=...; v=lo2+rng.random()*(hi2-lo2)
                 cand[i2]=v; cand[j2]=s2-v
              cand=project_box_sum(cand,target)
              f=objective_value(cand)
              if f < best_f - 1e-12:
                  best_h=cand; best_f=f; h=cand; no=0
              else:
                  h=best_h.copy(); no=0
    return best_h,best_f
```
This may evaluate many times per pair (4) increasing cost. Limit time 30s. For n=70, each pair 4 evals, maybe 1000 pairs/s? good. For n=300, 4*40k=160k ops, 200/s, okay 30s=6000 pairs.

Could be beneficial.

```
def register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h, diversity_tol=0.05):
    if h is None: return
    h2 = make_even_candidate(h)
    if h2 is None: return
    c = objective_value(h2)
    if not np.isfinite(c): return
    if c < state['c']:
       state['h'] = h2.copy(); state['c'] = float(c)
    oldmax = max(cand_c) if cand_c else 1e9
    if (len(candidates)==0 or c < oldmax + diversity_tol):
       # insert sorted
       idx=len(candidates)
       for i in range(len(candidates)):
          if c < cand_c[i]: idx=i; break
       candidates.insert(idx, h2.copy()); cand_c.insert(idx, float(c))
       if len(candidates)>10:
          pop last best? Since sorted ascending, last worst. Pop last.
```

`make_even_candidate(h)`:
```
def make_even_candidate(h):
   arr=np.asarray(h,float)
   if arr.size==0: return None
   if arr.size%2==1:
      if arr.size>1: arr=arr[:-1]
      else: arr=np.array([0.5,0.5])
   target=arr.size/2.0
   # If values are nearly binary but sum off? repair.
   return project_box_sum(arr,target)
```

`binarize` for fractional:
```
def binarize_candidate(h):
   n=h.size; m=n//2
   h2=(h >= 0.5).astype(float)
   ones=int(h2.sum())
   if ones>m:
      idx=np.where(h2==1.0)[0]
      order=np.argsort(h[idx]) # closest to 0.5 above
      for k in range(ones-m): h2[order[k]]=0.0
   elif ones<m:
      idx=np.where(h2==0.0)[0]
      order=np.argsort(-h[idx]) # closest to 0.5 below
      for k in range(m-ones): h2[order[k]]=1.0
   return h2
```
If h all values exactly 0/1 and ones==m, returns same.

Pseudo final:
```
def projected_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start, tau_end, alpha_start, seed):
    if h is None: return h,1e9
    h=project_box_sum(h, target).copy()
    n=h.size
    dx=2.0/n
    # init
    pos,neg=lag_values(h)
    fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    f=fcount*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
    if time_limit <=0: return best_h,best_f
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    alpha=alpha_start
    alpha_min=1e-5
    stalled=0
    tau=tau_start
    tau_reduce=0.99
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()-start > time_limit: break
       pos,neg=lag_values(h)
       fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
       f=fcount*dx
       if f < best_f - 1e-14:
          best_f=f; best_h=h.copy(); stalled=0
       else:
          stalled +=1
       if stalled > 80:
          sigma = 0.005 + 0.005 * min(5, stalled // 80)
          h = project_box_sum(best_h + sigma * (rng.random(n) - 0.5), target)
          pos,neg=lag_values(h)
          fcount=max(...); f=...
          if f < best_f - 1e-14: best...
          stalled=0
          alpha=max(alpha_min, alpha*0.7)
       tau_eff = max(tau_end, tau_start * (0.995 ** it))
       g, _ = smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau_eff)
       norm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       accepted=False
       if norm > 1e-12:
          gn=g/norm
          a=alpha
          for _ in range(5):
             hp=project_box_sum(h - a*gn, target)
             # quick if same as h? no
             f2=objective_value(hp)
             if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                h=hp; accepted=True; break
             a *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
          # active directions: top 2 pos, top2 neg
          for e in np.argsort(pos)[::-1][:3]:
             if norm not? For each:
                g2=grad_one(h,int(e),True)
                n2=float(np.linalg.norm(g2))
                if n2 <=1e-12: continue
                g2 /= n2
                a=alpha
                for _ in range(3):
                   hp=project_box_sum(h - a*g2, target)
                   f2=objective_value(hp)
                   if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                      h=hp; accepted=True; break
                   a*=0.5
                if accepted: break
          if not accepted:
             for e in np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]:
                if int(e)==0: continue
                ...
       if accepted:
          alpha = min(0.2, alpha*1.05)
       else:
          alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha*0.65)
          if stalled > 20 and it % 50 == 49:
             tau_start = max(tau_end, tau_start*0.85)
    return best_h,best_f
```

Define:
```
def markov_binary(n, gamma, seed):
   rng = RandomState(seed)
   h=np.empty(n)
   h[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand()<0.5 else 0.0
   for i in range(1,n):
      if h[i-1] > 0.5:
         h[i] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 0.0
      else:
         h[i] = 0.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 1.0
   m=n//2
   ones=int(h.sum())
   if ones>m:
      idx=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
      flip = idx[rng.choice(ones, ones-m, replace=False)]
      h[flip]=0.0
   elif ones<m:
      idx=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]
      flip = idx[rng.choice(n-ones, m-ones, replace=False)]
      h[flip]=1.0
   return h
```

def block_local_search(p, n_grid, time_limit, seed, initial_weights=None):
   n=n_grid (even)
   m=n/2
   best_h=None; best_f=1e9
   start=time
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   # initial lengths: maybe uniform with noise
   a = rng.dirichlet(p) * 1.0 # sum1
   b = rng.dirichlet(p+1) * 1.0
   current_h = build_blocks(a,b,p,n)
   current_f = objective_value(current_h)
   best=...
   while time<limit:
      # perturb one length randomly
      if rng.rand()< p/(2p+1): choose one length index, multiply factor exp(noise)
      else zero length
      a2=a*factor; normalize sum to1
      build, eval; if better accept; else reject.
      # Occasionally multi-length perturbation.
   return best_h,best_f
```

Code:
```
def normalize_group(arr, total):
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s <= 0: return arr * (total/len(arr))
   return arr * (total/s)

def build_blocks(a,b,p,n_grid):
   n=n_grid
   h=np.zeros(n)
   pos=0.0
   for i in range(p):
      pos += 2.0 * b[i]
      start = int(np.floor(pos * n))
      if start < 0: start=0
      pos += 2.0 * a[i]
      end = int(np.ceil(pos * n))
      if end > n: end=n
      if end > start:
          h[start:end] = 1.0
   # after loop, remaining gap sum 1 should reach pos=2
   return h

def adjust_binary_counts(h, m, seed):
   h=h.astype(float).copy()
   ones=int(np.sum(h>0.5))
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if ones>m:
      idx=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
      need=ones-m
      if need>0:
         flip=idx[rng.choice(need, need, replace=False)]? rng.choice array size.
```
Need `rng.choice(idx, need, replace=False)`. If need > len? no.
```
   elif ones<m:
      idx=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]
      need=m-ones
      flip=idx[rng.choice(need, need, replace=False)]
```
But `rng.choice(need, need, replace=False)` if array size need. Use `flip = idx[rng.choice(len(idx), need, replace=False)]`.

def block_local_search(p, n_grid, time_limit, seed, start_a=None, start_b=None):
   if n_grid %2: n_grid +=1
   m=n_grid/2.0
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if start_a is None: a=rng.random(p)+0.2? use dirichlet? Not have dirichlet? np.random.dirichlet available. Use `rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p))`. For small p ok.
   b=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p+1))
   a=normalize_group(a,1.0); b=normalize_group(b,1.0)

`fine_adjust_sum`:
```
def fine_adjust_sum(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(target)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(z))
    tol = 1e-14
    if diff > tol:
        order = np.argsort(z)[::-1]
        rem = diff
        for idx in order:
            space = 1.0 - z[idx]
            if space > 0.0:
                add = space if space < rem else rem
                z[idx] += add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= tol:
                    break
    elif diff < -tol:
        order = np.argsort(z)
        rem = -diff
        for idx in order:
            space = z[idx]
            if space > 0.0:
                sub = space if space < rem else rem
                z[idx] -= sub
                rem -= sub
                if rem <= tol:
                    break
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    return z
```
After adding, if z[idx]=1+1e-16 clipped back, sum may revert; but diff tiny. OK.

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(h,target):
    y=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    y=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
    target=float(np.clip(target,0.0,float(y.size)))
    if y.size==0: return y
    s0 = float(np.sum(y))
    if abs(s0-target) <= 1e-12:
        return fine_adjust_sum(y,target)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    nu=0.5*(lo+hi)
    z=np.clip(y-nu,0.0,1.0)
    return fine_adjust_sum(z,target)
```
If target not feasible? target within [0,n]. Good.

`objective_value`:
```
def objective_value(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return 1e9
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    val=float(np.max(corr))*dx
    if val < 0.0: val=0.0
    return val
```
If h contains nan, val nan; register checks finite.

`lag_values`:
```
def lag_values(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size
    oh=1.0-h
    pos=np.empty(n)
    neg=np.empty(n)
    pos[0]=float(np.dot(h,oh))
    neg[0]=pos[0]
    for e in range(1,n):
       d=n-e
       pos[e]=float(np.dot(h[:d], oh[e:]))
       neg[e]=float(np.dot(h[e:], oh[:d]))
    return pos,neg
```
For n=1, pos[0], neg[0]. But n even >=. OK.

`grad_one`:
```
def grad_one(h,e,positive):
    n=h.size; g=np.zeros(n); oh=1.0-h; d=n-e
    if positive:
       g[:d] += oh[e:]
       if e>0: g[e:] -= h[:d]
    else:
       g[e:] += oh[:d]
       g[:d] -= h[e:]
    return g
```
For e=0 pos: g[:]+oh; g[0:] -=h =>1-2h. neg same, but we skip neg0.

`smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,tau):
    n=h.size
    oh=1.0-h
    fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if tau <= 1e-14:
       return g, fcount
    wpos=np.exp(np.clip((pos-fcount)/tau, -40.0, 0.0))
    wneg=np.exp(np.clip((neg-fcount)/tau, -40.0, 0.0))
    wneg[0]=0.0
    W=float(wpos.sum()+wneg.sum())
    if W <= 0: return g,fcount
    maxw=max(float(wpos.max()), float(wneg.max()))
    thr=max(1e-9, 1e-6*maxw)
    idxp=np.where(wpos > thr)[0]
    for e in idxp:
       w=wpos[e]/W
       if w <= 0: continue
       d=n-e
       g[:d] += w*oh[e:]
       if e>0:
          g[e:] -= w*h[:d]
    idxn=np.where(wneg > thr)[0]
    for e in idxn:
       w=wneg[e]/W
       if w <=0: continue
       d=n-e
       g[e:] += w*oh[:d]
       g[:d] -= w*h[e:]
    return g, fcount
```
Note threshold `1e-6*maxw`; if maxw=1, skip weights <1e-6. Good. Using `np.clip` with -40 safe. If tau small, max fcount weights 1; lower underflow maybe. Good.

`auto_tau`:

def auto_tau(h):
    pos,neg=lag_values(h)
    fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    tau_s=max(1.0, 0.05*fcount)
    tau_e=max(0.2, 0.005*fcount)
    return tau_s,tau_e
```

`pairwise_random_search`: Need use `target` and h. It modifies h local. Use `objective_value` each candidate. To reduce copies, use `cand = h.copy()`.

```
def pairwise_random_search(h, target, time_limit, seed):
    h=project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    n=h.size
    best_h=h.copy()
    best_f=objective_value(h)
    if time_limit <=0: return best_h,best_f
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stall=0
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
       idxs=rng.choice(n, 2, replace=False)
       i=int(idxs[0]); j=int(idxs[1])
       s=h[i]+h[j]
       lo = s - 1.0
       if lo < 0.0: lo=0.0
       hi = s
       if hi > 1.0: hi=1.0
       if hi - lo <= 1e-15:
          stall +=1
       else:
          # candidate values: random, lo, hi, mid
          vals = [lo + rng.random()*(hi-lo), lo, hi, 0.5*(lo+hi)]
          for v in vals:
             vf=float(v)
             if vf < lo - 1e-12 or vf > hi + 1e-12: continue
             if vf < 0.0: vf=0.0
             if vf > 1.0: vf=1.0
             w=s-vf
             if w < 0.0: w=0.0
             if w > 1.0: w=1.0
             cand=h.copy()
             cand[i]=vf; cand[j]=w
             f=objective_value(cand)
             if f < best_f - 1e-12:
                 best_h=cand; best_f=f; h=cand; stall=0; break
          else? not in Python? Use accepted flag.
       if not accepted:
          stall +=1
          if stall > 500:
             # perturb best with 2 pairs
             cand=best_h.copy()
             for _ in range(2):
                idxs2=rng.choice(n,2,replace=False)
                i2=int...; j2=...
                s2=cand[i2]+cand[j2]
                lo2=max(0,s2-1); hi2=min(1,s2)
                if hi2-lo2>1e-15:
                   v=lo2+rng.random()*(hi2-lo2); cand[i2]=v; cand[j2]=s2-v
             cand=project_box_sum(cand,target)
             f=objective_value(cand)
             if f < best_f - 1e-12:
                 best_h=cand; best_f=f; h=cand
             stall=0
    return best_h,best_f
```
Need `accepted` initialization. We'll code.

`upsample_step`:
```
def upsample_step(h,n_new):
    n_old=h.size
    n_new=int(n_new)
    if n_new % 2: n_new +=1
    out=np.zeros(n_new)
    prev=0
    for i in range(n_old):
       end=(n_new*(i+1))//n_old
       if end < prev: end=prev
       out[prev:end]=h[i]
       prev=end
    if prev < n_new:
       out[prev:n_new]=h[-1]
    return project_box_sum(out, n_new/2.0)
```
If n_new < n_old, possible negative? We choose larger. If n_old=0? no.

`register_candidate`: Need keep candidates sorted. If state h None, state h. Need if candidate same length and c almost same? Avoid too many. We'll just cap and sorted.
```
def register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h, diversity_tol=0.05):
    if h is None: return
    h2=make_even_candidate(h)
    if h2 is None: return
    c=objective_value(h2)
    if not np.isfinite(c): return
    if c < state['c'] - 1e-14:
        state['c']=float(c); state['h']=h2.copy()
    if len(candidates)==0 or c < max(cand_c) + diversity_tol:
        # if there is an existing same length with significantly better c, skip? Not necessary.
        idx=len(candidates)
        for i in range(len(candidates)):
            if c < cand_c[i]:
                idx=i; break
        # avoid duplicates: if len same and c close to existing? skip
        for i in range(len(candidates)):
            if candidates[i].shape == h2.shape and abs(cand_c[i]-c) <= 1e-12:
                return
        candidates.insert(idx,h2.copy())
        cand_c.insert(idx,float(c))
        if len(candidates) > 10:
            pop=len(candidates)-1
            candidates.pop(pop); cand_c.pop(pop)
```
If state c equal but not improved and cand is within diversity, added. Good.

def make_even_candidate(h):
    arr=np.asarray(h,float)
    if arr.size==0: return None
    if arr.size %2 != 0:
        if arr.size > 1:
            arr=arr[:-1]
        else:
            arr=np.array([0.5,0.5], float)
    target=arr.size/2.0
    return project_box_sum(arr,target)
```

`binarize_candidate`:
```
def binarize_candidate(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).ravel()
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return None
    if n%2: n=n-1? Actually return even. Use make_even first? We'll assume even.
    m=n//2
    h2=(h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    ones=int(h2.sum())
    if ones > m:
       idx=np.where(h2 > 0.5)[0]
       need=ones-m
       if need>0 and need < len(idx):
          # choose ones closest to 0.5: smallest h among ones
          order=np.argsort(h[idx]); h2[idx[order[:need]]]=0.0
       elif need>0:
          h2[idx[:need]]=0.0
    elif ones < m:
       idx=np.where(h2 <= 0.5)[0]
       need=m-ones
       if need>0 and need < len(idx):
          order=np.argsort(-h[idx]); h2[idx[order[:need]]]=1.0
       elif need>0:
          h2[idx[:need]]=1.0
    return project_box_sum(h2,m)
```
If need=0, no. If idx empty? impossible if need>0? For ones>m, idx size ones≥need. For ones<m zeros size n-ones≥m-ones. Good. Use `idx[order[:need]]` array assignment.

`markov_binary`:
```
def markov_binary(n, gamma, seed):
    n=int(n)
    if n%2: n+=1
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=np.empty(n)
    h[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1,n):
       if h[i-1] > 0.5:
          h[i] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 0.0
       else:
          h[i] = 0.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 1.0
    return adjust_binary_counts(h, n//2, seed)
```
`adjust_binary_counts` used by markov/block.

`adjust_binary_counts`:
```
def adjust_binary_counts(h,m,seed):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).ravel().copy()
    m=int(m)
    if h.size != 2*m: ??? If n odd? We'll assume even.
    ones=int(np.sum(h>0.5))
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if ones > m:
       idx=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
       need=ones-m
       if need > 0:
          flip=idx[rng.choice(len(idx), need, replace=False)]
          h[flip]=0.0
    elif ones < m:
       idx=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]
       need=m-ones
       if need>0:
          flip=idx[rng.choice(len(idx), need, replace=False)]
          h[flip]=1.0
    return h
```
If len(idx)=0 and need>0 impossible for valid counts. Good.

`normalize_group`:
```
def normalize_group(arr,total):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float).copy()
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        arr[:] = total / float(arr.size)
        return arr
    arr = arr * (total / s)
    return arr
```

`build_blocks`:
```
def build_blocks(a,b,p,n_grid):
    h=np.zeros(n_grid)
    pos=0.0
    for i in range(p):
       pos += 2.0 * float(b[i])
       start=int(np.floor(pos * n_grid))
       if start < 0: start=0
       pos += 2.0 * float(a[i])
       end=int(np.ceil(pos * n_grid))
       if end > n_grid: end=n_grid
       if end > start:
          h[start:end] = 1.0
    return h
```
Maybe after last gap, pos=2. Good.

`block_local_search` code:
```
def block_local_search(p, n_grid, time_limit, seed):
    if time_limit <=0: return None, 1e9
    n_grid=int(n_grid)
    if n_grid %2: n_grid +=1
    m=n_grid//2
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    a=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p))
    b=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p+1))
    a=normalize_group(a,1.0); b=normalize_group(b,1.0)
    h=adjust_binary_counts(build_blocks(a,b,p,n_grid), m, seed)
    cur_a=a.copy(); cur_b=b.copy(); cur_h=h.copy(); cur_f=objective_value(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=cur_f; best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy()
    sigma=0.25
    stall=0
    start=time.time()
    k=0
    while time.time()-start < time_limit:
       k += 1
       factor = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
       cur_a2=cur_a.copy(); cur_b2=cur_b.copy()
       if rng.rand() < p / float(2*p+1):
          idx=int(rng.randint(cur_a2.size))
          cur_a2[idx] *= factor
          cur_a2 = normalize_group(cur_a2,1.0)
       else:
          idx=int(rng.randint(cur_b2.size))
          cur_b2[idx] *= factor
          cur_b2 = normalize_group(cur_b2,1.0)
       cand = adjust_binary_counts(build_blocks(cur_a2, cur_b2, p, n_grid), m, seed + k)
       f2=objective_value(cand)
       if f2 < cur_f - 1e-13:
          cur_a=cur_a2; cur_b=cur_b2; cur_h=cand; cur_f=f2
          if f2 < best_f - 1e-13:
             best_h=cand.copy(); best_f=f2; best_a=cur_a2.copy(); best_b=cur_b2.copy()
          stall=0
       else:
          stall +=1
          if stall > 80:
             cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_b=best_b.copy(); cur_h=best_h.copy(); cur_f=best_f
             sigma = max(0.02, sigma*0.6)
             stall=0
       if k % 200 == 199:
          sigma = max(0.02, sigma*0.97)
    return best_h, best_f
```
Potential: `rng.rand() < p/(2p+1)` probability. If p=5, 5/11. Good.

```
def projected_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start, tau_end, alpha_start, seed):
    h=project_box_sum(h,target).copy()
    n=h.size
    dx=2.0/n
    pos,neg=lag_values(h)
    fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    f=fcount*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_f=f
    if time_limit <=0: return best_h,best_f
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    alpha=alpha_start
    alpha_min=1e-5
    tau=tau_start
    stalled=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.time()-start > time_limit: break
        pos,neg=lag_values(h)
        fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
        f=fcount*dx
        if f < best_f - 1e-14:
            best_f=f; best_h=h.copy(); stalled=0
        else:
            stalled += 1
        if stalled > 80:
            sigma = 0.005 + 0.005 * min(5, stalled // 80)
            h = project_box_sum(best_h + sigma * (rng.random(n) - 0.5), target)
            pos,neg=lag_values(h)
            fcount=max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
            f=fcount*dx
            if f < best_f - 1e-14:
                best_f=f; best_h=h.copy()
            stalled=0
            alpha=max(alpha_min, alpha*0.7)
        g, _ = smooth_gradient(h,pos,neg,max(tau_end,tau))
        norm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        accepted=False
        if norm > 1e-12:
            gn=g/norm
            a=alpha
            for _ in range(5):
                hp=project_box_sum(h - a*gn, target)
                f2=objective_value(hp)
                if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                    h=hp; accepted=True; break
                a *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            # pos active
            for e in np.argsort(pos)[::-1][:3]:
                e=int(e)
                g2=grad_one(h,e,True)
                n2=float(np.linalg.norm(g2))
                if n2 <= 1e-14: continue
                g2=g2/n2
                a=alpha; ok=False
                for _ in range(3):
                    hp=project_box_sum(h - a*g2, target)
                    f2=objective_value(hp)
                    if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                        h=hp; ok=True; break
                    a *= 0.5
                if ok:
                    accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            for e in np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]:
                e=int(e)
                if e == 0: continue
                g2=grad_one(h,e,False)
                n2=float(np.linalg.norm(g2))
                if n2 <=1e-14: continue
                g2=g2/n2
                a=alpha; ok=False
                for _ in range(3):
                    hp=project_box_sum(h - a*g2, target)
                    f2=objective_value(hp)
                    if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                        h=hp; ok=True; break
                    a *= 0.5
                if ok:
                    accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
            alpha = min(0.2, alpha*1.05)
        else:
            alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha*0.65)
        tau = max(tau_end, tau*0.995)
        if stalled > 40 and it % 100 == 99:
            tau = max(tau_end, tau*0.85)
    return best_h,best_f
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def fine_adjust_sum(z, target):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(target)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(z))
    tol = 1e-14
    if diff > tol:
        order = np.argsort(z)[::-1]
        rem = diff
        for idx in order:
            space = 1.0 - z[idx]
            if space > 0.0:
                add = space if space < rem else rem
                z[idx] += add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= tol:
                    break
    elif diff < -tol:
        order = np.argsort(z)
        rem = -diff
        for idx in order:
            space = z[idx]
            if space > 0.0:
                sub = space if space < rem else rem
                z[idx] -= sub
                rem -= sub
                if rem <= tol:
                    break
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    return z


def project_box_sum(h, target):
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(np.clip(target, 0.0, float(y.size)))
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    s0 = float(np.sum(y))
    if abs(s0 - target) <= 1e-12:
        return fine_adjust_sum(y, target)
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    nu = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    z = np.clip(y - nu, 0.0, 1.0)
    return fine_adjust_sum(z, target)


def objective_value(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1e9
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    val = float(np.max(corr)) * dx
    if val < 0.0:
        val = 0.0
    return val


def lag_values(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    oh = 1.0 - h
    pos = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    neg = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    pos[0] = float(np.dot(h, oh))
    neg[0] = pos[0]
    for e in range(1, n):
        d = n - e
        pos[e] = float(np.dot(h[:d], oh[e:]))
        neg[e] = float(np.dot(h[e:], oh[:d]))
    return pos, neg


def grad_one(h, e, positive):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    oh = 1.0 - h
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    d = n - e
    if positive:
        g[:d] += oh[e:]
        if e > 0:
            g[e:] -= h[:d]
    else:
        g[e:] += oh[:d]
        g[:d] -= h[e:]
    return g


def smooth_gradient(h, pos, neg, tau):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    oh = 1.0 - h
    fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if tau <= 1e-14:
        return g, fcount
    wpos = np.exp(np.clip((pos - fcount) / tau, -40.0, 0.0))
    wneg = np.exp(np.clip((neg - fcount) / tau, -40.0, 0.0))
    wneg[0] = 0.0
    W = float(wpos.sum() + wneg.sum())
    if W <= 0.0:
        return g, fcount
    maxw = max(float(wpos.max()), float(wneg.max()))
    thr = max(1e-9, 1e-6 * maxw)
    idxp = np.where(wpos > thr)[0]
    for e in idxp:
        w = wpos[e] / W
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        d = n - e
        g[:d] += w * oh[e:]
        if e > 0:
            g[e:] -= w * h[:d]
    idxn = np.where(wneg > thr)[0]
    for e in idxn:
        w = wneg[e] / W
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        d = n - e
        g[e:] += w * oh[:d]
        g[:d] -= w * h[e:]
    return g, fcount


def auto_tau(h):
    pos, neg = lag_values(h)
    fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    tau_s = max(1.0, 0.05 * fcount)
    tau_e = max(0.2, 0.005 * fcount)
    return tau_s, tau_e


def projected_refine(h, target, max_iter, time_limit, tau_start, tau_end, alpha_start, seed):
    if h is None:
        return None, 1e9
    h = project_box_sum(h, target).copy()
    n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    pos, neg = lag_values(h)
    fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
    f = fcount * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = f
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return best_h, best_f
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    alpha = float(alpha_start)
    alpha_min = 1e-5
    tau = float(tau_start)
    stalled = 0
    for it in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() - start > float(time_limit):
            break
        pos, neg = lag_values(h)
        fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
        f = fcount * dx
        if f < best_f - 1e-14:
            best_f = f
            best_h = h.copy()
            stalled = 0
        else:
            stalled += 1
        if stalled > 80:
            sigma = 0.005 + 0.005 * min(5, stalled // 80)
            h = project_box_sum(best_h + sigma * (rng.random(n) - 0.5), target)
            pos, neg = lag_values(h)
            fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
            f = fcount * dx
            if f < best_f - 1e-14:
                best_f = f
                best_h = h.copy()
            stalled = 0
            alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha * 0.7)
        g, _ = smooth_gradient(h, pos, neg, max(tau_end, tau))
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        accepted = False
        if norm > 1e-12:
            gn = g / norm
            a = alpha
            for _ in range(5):
                hp = project_box_sum(h - a * gn, target)
                f2 = objective_value(hp)
                if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                    h = hp
                    accepted = True
                    break
                a *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            for e in np.argsort(pos)[::-1][:3]:
                e = int(e)
                g2 = grad_one(h, e, True)
                n2 = float(np.linalg.norm(g2))
                if n2 <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                g2 = g2 / n2
                a = alpha
                ok = False
                for _ in range(3):
                    hp = project_box_sum(h - a * g2, target)
                    f2 = objective_value(hp)
                    if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                        h = hp
                        ok = True
                        break
                    a *= 0.5
                if ok:
                    accepted = True
                    break
        if not accepted:
            for e in np.argsort(neg)[::-1][:3]:
                e = int(e)
                if e == 0:
                    continue
                g2 = grad_one(h, e, False)
                n2 = float(np.linalg.norm(g2))
                if n2 <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                g2 = g2 / n2
                a = alpha
                ok = False
                for _ in range(3):
                    hp = project_box_sum(h - a * g2, target)
                    f2 = objective_value(hp)
                    if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                        h = hp
                        ok = True
                        break
                    a *= 0.5
                if ok:
                    accepted = True
                    break
        if accepted:
            alpha = min(0.2, alpha * 1.05)
        else:
            alpha = max(alpha_min, alpha * 0.65)
        tau = max(tau_end, tau * 0.995)
        if stalled > 40 and (it % 100) == 99:
            tau = max(tau_end, tau * 0.85)
    return best_h, best_f


def pairwise_random_search(h, target, time_limit, seed):
    h = project_box_sum(h, target).copy()
    n = h.size
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = objective_value(h)
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return best_h, best_f
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stall = 0
    while time.time() - start < float(time_limit):
        idxs = rng.choice(n, 2, replace=False)
        i = int(idxs[0])
        j = int(idxs[1])
        s = h[i] + h[j]
        lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
        hi = min(1.0, s)
        accepted = False
        if hi - lo > 1e-15:
            vals = [lo + rng.random() * (hi - lo), lo, hi, 0.5 * (lo + hi)]
            for v in vals:
                vf = float(v)
                if vf < lo - 1e-12 or vf > hi + 1e-12:
                    continue
                if vf < 0.0:
                    vf = 0.0
                if vf > 1.0:
                    vf = 1.0
                w = s - vf
                if w < 0.0:
                    w = 0.0
                if w > 1.0:
                    w = 1.0
                cand = h.copy()
                cand[i] = vf
                cand[j] = w
                f = objective_value(cand)
                if f < best_f - 1e-12:
                    best_h = cand
                    best_f = f
                    h = cand
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True
                    break
        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 500:
                cand = best_h.copy()
                for _ in range(2):
                    idxs2 = rng.choice(n, 2, replace=False)
                    i2 = int(idxs2[0])
                    j2 = int(idxs2[1])
                    s2 = cand[i2] + cand[j2]
                    lo2 = max(0.0, s2 - 1.0)
                    hi2 = min(1.0, s2)
                    if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-15:
                        v2 = lo2 + rng.random() * (hi2 - lo2)
                        cand[i2] = v2
                        cand[j2] = s2 - v2
                cand = project_box_sum(cand, target)
                f = objective_value(cand)
                if f < best_f - 1e-12:
                    best_h = cand
                    best_f = f
                    h = cand
                stall = 0
    return best_h, best_f


def upsample_step(h, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = h.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_new % 2 != 0:
        n_new += 1
    out = np.zeros(n_new, dtype=float)
    prev = 0
    for i in range(n_old):
        end = (n_new * (i + 1)) // n_old
        if end < prev:
            end = prev
        out[prev:end] = h[i]
        prev = end
    if prev < n_new:
        out[prev:n_new] = h[-1]
    return project_box_sum(out, n_new / 2.0)


def make_even_candidate(h):
    arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if arr.size % 2 != 0:
        if arr.size > 1:
            arr = arr[:-1]
        else:
            arr = np.array([0.5, 0.5], dtype=float)
    target = arr.size / 2.0
    return project_box_sum(arr, target)


def symmetrize(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return (h + h[::-1]) * 0.5


def binarize_candidate(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if h.size % 2 != 0:
        h = h[:-1]
    if h.size == 0:
        return None
    n = h.size
    m = n // 2
    h2 = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    ones = int(h2.sum())
    if ones > m:
        idx = np.where(h2 > 0.5)[0]
        need = ones - m
        if need > 0:
            order = np.argsort(h[idx])
            h2[idx[order[:need]]] = 0.0
    elif ones < m:
        idx = np.where(h2 <= 0.5)[0]
        need = m - ones
        if need > 0:
            order = np.argsort(-h[idx])
            h2[idx[order[:need]]] = 1.0
    return project_box_sum(h2, m)


def adjust_binary_counts(h, m, seed):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    m = int(m)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    ones = int(np.sum(h > 0.5))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if ones > m:
        idx = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        need = ones - m
        if need > 0 and idx.size > 0:
            flip = idx[rng.choice(idx.size, need, replace=False)]
            h[flip] = 0.0
    elif ones < m:
        idx = np.where(h <= 0.5)[0]
        need = m - ones
        if need > 0 and idx.size > 0:
            flip = idx[rng.choice(idx.size, need, replace=False)]
            h[flip] = 1.0
    return h


def markov_binary(n, gamma, seed):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        if h[i - 1] > 0.5:
            h[i] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 0.0
        else:
            h[i] = 0.0 if rng.rand() < gamma else 1.0
    return adjust_binary_counts(h, n // 2, seed)


def normalize_group(arr, total):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).copy()
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        arr[:] = total / float(arr.size)
        return arr
    arr = arr * (total / s)
    return arr


def build_blocks(a, b, p, n_grid):
    h = np.zeros(int(n_grid), dtype=float)
    n_grid = h.size
    pos = 0.0
    for i in range(p):
        pos += 2.0 * float(b[i])
        start = int(np.floor(pos * n_grid))
        if start < 0:
            start = 0
        pos += 2.0 * float(a[i])
        end = int(np.ceil(pos * n_grid))
        if end > n_grid:
            end = n_grid
        if end > start:
            h[start:end] = 1.0
    return h


def block_local_search(p, n_grid, time_limit, seed):
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return None, 1e9
    p = int(p)
    n_grid = int(n_grid)
    if n_grid % 2 != 0:
        n_grid += 1
    m = n_grid // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    a = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p, dtype=float))
    b = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p + 1, dtype=float))
    a = normalize_group(a, 1.0)
    b = normalize_group(b, 1.0)
    h0 = adjust_binary_counts(build_blocks(a, b, p, n_grid), m, seed)
    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_b = b.copy()
    cur_h = h0.copy()
    cur_f = objective_value(cur_h)
    best_h = cur_h.copy()
    best_f = cur_f
    best_a = cur_a.copy()
    best_b = cur_b.copy()
    sigma = 0.25
    stall = 0
    start = time.time()
    k = 0
    while time.time() - start < float(time_limit):
        k += 1
        factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        cur_a2 = cur_a.copy()
        cur_b2 = cur_b.copy()
        if rng.rand() < (float(p) / float(2 * p + 1)):
            idx = int(rng.randint(cur_a2.size))
            cur_a2[idx] *= factor
            cur_a2 = normalize_group(cur_a2, 1.0)
        else:
            idx = int(rng.randint(cur_b2.size))
            cur_b2[idx] *= factor
            cur_b2 = normalize_group(cur_b2, 1.0)
        cand = adjust_binary_counts(build_blocks(cur_a2, cur_b2, p, n_grid), m, seed + k)
        f2 = objective_value(cand)
        if f2 < cur_f - 1e-13:
            cur_a = cur_a2
            cur_b = cur_b2
            cur_h = cand
            cur_f = f2
            if f2 < best_f - 1e-13:
                best_f = f2
                best_h = cand.copy()
                best_a = cur_a2.copy()
                best_b = cur_b2.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 80:
                cur_a = best_a.copy()
                cur_b = best_b.copy()
                cur_h = best_h.copy()
                cur_f = best_f
                sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.6)
                stall = 0
        if (k % 200) == 199:
            sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.97)
    return best_h, best_f


def register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h, diversity_tol=0.05):
    if h is None:
        return
    h2 = make_even_candidate(h)
    if h2 is None:
        return
    c = objective_value(h2)
    if not np.isfinite(c):
        return
    if c < state["c"] - 1e-14:
        state["c"] = float(c)
        state["h"] = h2.copy()
    if len(candidates) == 0 or c < max(cand_c) + float(diversity_tol):
        for i in range(len(candidates)):
            if candidates[i].shape == h2.shape and abs(cand_c[i] - c) <= 1e-12:
                return
        idx = len(candidates)
        for i in range(len(candidates)):
            if c < cand_c[i]:
                idx = i
                break
        candidates.insert(idx, h2.copy())
        cand_c.insert(idx, float(c))
        if len(candidates) > 10:
            candidates.pop()
            cand_c.pop()


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    state = {"h": None, "c": 1e9}
    candidates = []
    cand_c = []
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    if "initial_h_values" in globals():
        base = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, base)
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, symmetrize(base))
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, binarize_candidate(base))
        base_n = state["h"].size if state["h"] is not None else 70
        for gamma in (0.9, 0.8, 0.7):
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, markov_binary(base_n, gamma, seed + 30))

    if state["h"] is None:
        for nn in (70, 140):
            for gamma in (0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.5):
                register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, markov_binary(nn, gamma, seed + 50))
        if state["h"] is None:
            arr = np.full(70, 0.5, dtype=float)
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, arr)

    if state["h"] is not None:
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if rem < 12.0:
            h = make_even_candidate(state["h"])
            return h, objective_value(h), h.size

    rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
    if state["h"] is not None and rem > 180.0:
        base_n = state["h"].size
        n_block = 280
        if base_n > 220:
            n_block = base_n
        for p, alloc in ((5, 35.0), (4, 22.0), (3, 14.0)):
            rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
            if rem < alloc + 45.0:
                break
            hb, cb = block_local_search(p, n_block, alloc, seed + 700 + p)
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, hb, diversity_tol=0.15)

    for ci in range(len(candidates)):
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if rem < 70.0:
            break
        if cand_c[ci] > state["c"] + 0.02:
            break
        h = candidates[ci]
        n = h.size
        m = n / 2.0
        tau_s, tau_e = auto_tau(h)
        if ci == 0:
            alloc = 240.0
        else:
            alloc = 90.0
        if n > 220:
            alloc = min(alloc, 80.0)
        elif n > 120:
            alloc = min(alloc, 140.0)
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, rem - 35.0))
        h2, c2 = projected_refine(
            h, m,
            max_iter=50000,
            time_limit=alloc,
            tau_start=tau_s,
            tau_end=tau_e,
            alpha_start=0.05,
            seed=seed + 100 + ci,
        )
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h2)
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if ci == 0 and rem > 75.0 and state["h"] is not None:
            mstate = state["h"].size / 2.0
            h3, c3 = pairwise_random_search(state["h"], mstate, min(30.0, rem - 35.0), seed + 222)
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h3)

    for n_new in (98, 140, 200, 280):
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        if state["h"] is None:
            break
        if state["h"].size >= n_new:
            continue
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        hup = upsample_step(state["h"], n_new)
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, hup)
        tau_s, tau_e = auto_tau(hup)
        if n_new <= 140:
            alloc = 160.0
        elif n_new <= 220:
            alloc = 100.0
        else:
            alloc = 70.0
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, rem - 45.0))
        h2, c2 = projected_refine(
            hup, n_new / 2.0,
            max_iter=50000,
            time_limit=alloc,
            tau_start=tau_s,
            tau_end=tau_e,
            alpha_start=0.05,
            seed=seed + 322 + n_new,
        )
        register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h2)
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        if rem > 55.0 and state["h"] is not None and state["h"].size == n_new:
            h3, c3 = pairwise_random_search(state["h"], n_new / 2.0, min(20.0, rem - 30.0), seed + 422 + n_new)
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h3)

    if state["h"] is not None:
        base = state["h"]
        m = base.size / 2.0
        for k in range(3):
            rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
            if rem < 45.0:
                break
            noise = 0.02 + 0.01 * k
            hnoise = project_box_sum(base + noise * (rng.random(base.size) - 0.5), m)
            tau_s, tau_e = auto_tau(hnoise)
            h2, c2 = projected_refine(
                hnoise, m,
                max_iter=20000,
                time_limit=28.0,
                tau_start=tau_s,
                tau_end=tau_e,
                alpha_start=0.03,
                seed=seed + 522 + k,
            )
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h2)

    if state["h"] is not None:
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        m = state["h"].size / 2.0
        if rem > 55.0:
            h2, c2 = pairwise_random_search(state["h"], m, min(45.0, rem - 30.0), seed + 622)
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h2)

    if state["h"] is not None:
        rem = budget_s - (time.time() - t0)
        m = state["h"].size / 2.0
        if rem > 50.0:
            pos, neg = lag_values(state["h"])
            fcount = max(float(pos.max()), float(neg.max()))
            tau_s = max(0.3, 0.02 * fcount)
            tau_e = max(0.1, 0.002 * fcount)
            tlim = max(18.0, rem - 30.0)
            h2, c2 = projected_refine(
                state["h"], m,
                max_iter=100000,
                time_limit=tlim,
                tau_start=tau_s,
                tau_end=tau_e,
                alpha_start=0.02,
                seed=seed + 722,
            )
            register_candidate(state, candidates, cand_c, h2)

    if state["h"] is None:
        h = make_even_candidate(np.full(70, 0.5, dtype=float))
        return h, objective_value(h), h.size

    h = make_even_candidate(state["h"])
    c = objective_value(h)
    if not np.isfinite(c):
        h = np.full(70, 0.5, dtype=float)
        c = objective_value(h)
    return h, c, h.size
```
